# Qwen3 encyclopedia work journal

Read the [loop plan](encyclopedia-local-model-plan.md) and [resume ledger](encyclopedia-qwen3-progress.json) before adding an entry. Append one entry per accepted or blocked unit. Keep exact source revision or digest, paths and line/record anchors, claim decision, changed files, coverage delta, checks, uncertainty, and next unit. Never mark a planned unit as completed without its source review and exit evidence.

## 2026-09-28 — handoff baseline

- State: plan and ledger prepared; no Qwen3 unit has run.
- Source heads recorded in the ledger. Both repositories contain prior uncommitted work; dirty-file digests and loaded-script scope still need to be captured at the first run.
- Next unit: `E0-loaded-source-manifest` (loaded-script manifest + dirty-file digests), then `E0-baseline-and-claim-audit`.
